## LLM Infrastructure

### [v0.26.1rc0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.26.1rc0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>[CI][ROCm] Fix <code>test_ocp_mx_wikitext_correctness</code> reference value (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/4">#4</a>…</p>

---

### [v0.26.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.26.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.26.0 Release Notes</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 411 commits from 212 contributors (61 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>New Inkling model family</strong> with a full support stack: base modeling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48799">#48799</a>), piecewise CUDA graph support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48822">#48822</a>), Hopper FA4 relative attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48858">#48858</a>), MTP=1 speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48869">#48869</a>), LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48884">#48884</a>), and standard ModelOpt NVFP4 quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48990">#48990</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4 performance push</strong> across vendors: a specialized routing kernel (2.94% E2E TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48660">#48660</a>), <code>fused_topk_bias</code> (1.5–2x kernel, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47463">#47463</a>), and redundant repeat/copy removal (1.8% E2E TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48137">#48137</a>), plus ROCm two-stage compressor for HCA prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47718">#47718</a>), sparse decode/prefill optimizations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48519">#48519</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48788">#48788</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46275">#46275</a>), and DSpark speculative decoding on AMD (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47419">#47419</a>) and XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47677">#47677</a>).</li>
<li><strong>fp32 <code>lm_head</code> for generation models via <code>head_dtype</code></strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48390">#48390</a>), extended to the LoRA path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48525">#48525</a>) and given a ROCm <code>torch.mm</code> fast path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48688">#48688</a>), improving accuracy for generation heads.</li>
<li><strong>Flexible attention backends</strong>: the attention backend can now be selected per KV-cache group (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48012">#48012</a>), and sliding-window support is now an explicit backend capability (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48011">#48011</a>) — improving support for hybrid models.</li>
<li><strong>KV offloading &amp; tiered secondary storage</strong> matured substantially: offloading metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45958">#45958</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47666">#47666</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47679">#47679</a>), tier-owned event handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46544">#46544</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47923">#47923</a>), object-store secondary tier with workload identity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47063">#47063</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47274">#47274</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48150">#48150</a>), DP-replica-aware tiering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47987">#47987</a>), and encoder-cache (EC) connectors including CPU offloading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42433">#42433</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47423">#47423</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend</strong> gained multimodal video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47959">#47959</a>) and audio (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48554">#48554</a>), a Seed-OSS tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47741">#47741</a>), and a native <code>vllm-bench</code> port (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48107">#48107</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers 5.13.0</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47867">#47867</a>) with more models migrated to the Transformers modeling backend: Olmo/Olmo2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48100">#48100</a>), MistralLarge3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48153">#48153</a>), and HunyuanVL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47872">#47872</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>New models: Inkling family (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48799">#48799</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48822">#48822</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48858">#48858</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48869">#48869</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48884">#48884</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48990">#48990</a>), BertForMaskedLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48463">#48463</a>), RobertaForTokenClassification / XLMRobertaForTokenClassification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47991">#47991</a>), LongCat-Flash-Lite n-gram embedding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47857">#47857</a>), Cosmos3 Edge Reasoner (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48291">#48291</a>) and Cosmos3-Super registration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48211">#48211</a>), TranslateGemma-12b-it (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41599">#41599</a>).</li>
<li>Transformers backend migrations: Olmo/Olmo2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48100">#48100</a>), MistralLarge3 to AutoWeightsLoader (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48153">#48153</a>), HunyuanVL native transformers processor for transformers 5.13 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47872">#47872</a>).</li>
<li>GLM5.2: migrate MoE sequence-parallel support to the non-torch-compiled path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47881">#47881</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA: FlashInfer MoE LoRA for BF16 models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48632">#48632</a>), LoRA for tower/connector in LlavaNextVideo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48594">#48594</a>), fp32 <code>lm_head</code> on the LoRA path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48525">#48525</a>), optimized <code>TrtLlmLoRAExperts</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48759">#48759</a>).</li>
<li>Multimodal: automatic fallback to ViT data parallelism when TP is unavailable (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49046">#49046</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: correct pooling scores for chunked prefill under <code>torch.compile</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48901">#48901</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li>fp32 <code>lm_head</code> for generation models via <code>head_dtype</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48390">#48390</a>); lower memory for capturing large CUDA graph sizes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48483">#48483</a>); opt-in persistence and reuse of the memory-profiling result across boots (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47388">#47388</a>); improved InstantTensor loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46868">#46868</a>).</li>
<li>Attention: select a different attention backend per KV-cache group (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48012">#48012</a>); sliding-window as an explicit backend capability (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48011">#48011</a>); KV-cache layout refactor packing K/V into the content dim across backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44455">#44455</a>); MRV2 virtual-batch PCP for MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46570">#46570</a>).</li>
<li>Speculative decoding: runtime draft weight update (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46725">#46725</a>), hybrid (SWA + full attention) DFlash drafters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47914">#47914</a>), SWA support for qwen-eagle3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47568">#47568</a>), Gemma4-12B DSpark draft model (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47216">#47216</a>), DSv4 DSpark on AMD (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47419">#47419</a>), separate <code>kv_cache_dtype</code> for <code>speculative_config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48787">#48787</a>).</li>
<li>KV offloading: basic offloading metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45958">#45958</a>), split CPU cache usage into read/write gauges (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47666">#47666</a>) and tiering-lookup-delay into sync/async histograms (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47679">#47679</a>), tier-owned event handling and BlockStored events (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46544">#46544</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47923">#47923</a>), object-store secondary tier with workload identity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47063">#47063</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47274">#47274</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48150">#48150</a>), DP-replica-aware tiering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47987">#47987</a>), <code>blocks_per_chunk</code> config for heterogeneous KV groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48878">#48878</a>), P2P default host/port env vars (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47636">#47636</a>).</li>
<li>Caching: partial prefix-cache hit for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46384">#46384</a>), selective hybrid cache retention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47782">#47782</a>), report prefix-cache-reused blocks in full report mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45261">#45261</a>).</li>
<li>Reasoning: optimize TPOT for thinking budget when used with speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46662">#46662</a>).</li>
<li>RLHF: stateful trainer-send abstractions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48042">#48042</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: host memory leak from undrained <code>new_block_ids</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44490">#44490</a>), DSv3.2 + MTP + sequence-parallel accuracy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48036">#48036</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li>DeepSeek-V4: specialized routing kernel (2.94% E2E TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48660">#48660</a>), <code>fused_topk_bias</code> 1.5–2x (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47463">#47463</a>), redundant repeat/copy removal (1.8% TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48137">#48137</a>).</li>
<li>MoE router GEMMs: BF16x3 router GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47973">#47973</a>), FP32 router GEMV (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48335">#48335</a>), generic CuteDSL LL BF16 router GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42562">#42562</a>); TRTLLM BF16 MoE modular kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45182">#45182</a>); write FlashInfer combine into final output (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47156">#47156</a>).</li>
<li>Qwen: fuse more RMSNorm + all-reduce in Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46998">#46998</a>), replace MoE all-reduce with reduce-scatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47006">#47006</a>), Qwen3.5 H20 optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48350">#48350</a>), expand Triton warmup coverage (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47546">#47546</a>).</li>
<li>MLA: dense MHA path for short sparse-MLA sequences (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47327">#47327</a>); MiniMax-M3 long-context decode indexer on sm100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48582">#48582</a>).</li>
<li>Kernels: CUDA kernel for ReLUSquaredActivation / relu^2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39058">#39058</a>), Helion kernel lazy registration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48264">#48264</a>), vectorize <code>_copy_mamba_state_block</code> to uint64 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48110">#48110</a>), stop upcasting logits to fp32 in the sampler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48641">#48641</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: fp32 <code>head_dtype</code> <code>torch.mm</code> fast path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48688">#48688</a>), DSv4 two-stage compressor kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47718">#47718</a>), sparse decode/prefill optimizations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48519">#48519</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48788">#48788</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46275">#46275</a>), DSv3.2 sparse MLA KV-split heuristic (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46832">#46832</a>) and MTP CUDA-graph mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45149">#45149</a>), MXFP8 GEMM for MiniMax-M3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46117">#46117</a>), AITER sparse paged attention + spec decode for MiniMax-M3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47287">#47287</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47984">#47984</a>), MiniMax-M2 fused QK-norm + all-reduce via AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44849">#44849</a>), HybridW4A16 linear kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40977">#40977</a>), Qwen3-30B-A3B QK-Norm+RoPE+KV runtime fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42749">#42749</a>).</li>
<li>XPU: batch-invariant kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41934">#41934</a>), HND KV layout support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47975">#47975</a>), DSpark spec decode for DSv4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47677">#47677</a>), nightly/release image publishing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47880">#47880</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48126">#48126</a>).</li>
<li>CPU: DFlash speculative decoding for GDN models on CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46090">#46090</a>), s390x NUMA topology (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40714">#40714</a>), native macOS arm64 CPU wheel builds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48289">#48289</a>); POWER VSX math function optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47321">#47321</a>) and IBM Power docker builds using prebuilt wheels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46017">#46017</a>).</li>
<li>Distributed fusion: FlashInfer MNNVL all-reduce RMS quant fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48064">#48064</a>).</li>
<li>Build/autotune: arm64 Blackwell SM10x/SM110 image builds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48041">#48041</a>); skip CuTeDSL fp4_gemm autotuning by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48268">#48268</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving &amp; Distributed</h3>
<ul>
<li>Decode Context Parallel (DCP): hybrid attention support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40996">#40996</a>), DCP + Eagle for Tokenspeed MLA backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48180">#48180</a>).</li>
<li>PD disaggregation: NIXL pipeline-parallel prefill in push mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45880">#45880</a>).</li>
<li>Encoder-cache connectors: EC transfer params (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42433">#42433</a>) and CPU-offloading EC connector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47423">#47423</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li>Humming w[2-7]a[4,8] weight-only inference with compressed-tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46390">#46390</a>); int4 quantization for the emulation MoE backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48451">#48451</a>); INT2 XPU weight-only quant linear (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47521">#47521</a>).</li>
<li>NVFP4/MXFP4: <code>nvfp4_per_token</code> online MoE quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48538">#48538</a>), CuTe-DSL FlashInfer MXFP4 quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48417">#48417</a>); bounded peak memory when repacking FP4 MoE weights for Marlin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47851">#47851</a>) and for NVFP4 MoE weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46276">#46276</a>).</li>
<li>MLA: <code>kv_cache_dtype_skip_layers</code> support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47309">#47309</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: HybridW4A16 linear kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40977">#40977</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li>Rust frontend: multimodal video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47959">#47959</a>) and audio (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48554">#48554</a>), Seed-OSS tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47741">#47741</a>), native <code>vllm-bench</code> port (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48107">#48107</a>), <code>continue_final_message</code> handling with renderer sentinel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47844">#47844</a>).</li>
<li>OpenAI compatibility: <code>bad_words</code> in <code>/v1/completions</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46793">#46793</a>), expose <code>logprob_token_ids</code> on Python OpenAI endpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43463">#43463</a>), <code>include_reasoning</code> param for non-Harmony models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44301">#44301</a>), populate <code>num_cache_creation_tokens</code> on Messages responses (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48535">#48535</a>).</li>
<li>Endpoint plugins framework (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47454">#47454</a>); <code>/abort_requests</code> on the RLHF dev API router (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47173">#47173</a>); Deepstream video decoding backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42424">#42424</a>); overlap preprocessing and computation for pooling models in offline inference (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47699">#47699</a>).</li>
<li>UX: human-readable integers for more CLI args (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47608">#47608</a>), CuTeDSL compilation progress bar (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48881">#48881</a>), expanded GPU profiler config scope/annotations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37524">#37524</a>), log worker exit code when a process dies unexpectedly (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38641">#38641</a>).</li>
<li>Stability/correctness: handle grammar compilation failures without crashing the engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47312">#47312</a>), fix logprobs token-string collision from SentencePiece spaces (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48674">#48674</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>Replace diskcache to eliminate pickle deserialization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44549">#44549</a>).</li>
<li>Fix a concurrent sparse-invariant race that bypassed CVE remediation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48583">#48583</a>).</li>
<li>Add resource-bounds validation to derender endpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47260">#47260</a>); sanitize server file paths from validation error responses (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46415">#46415</a>); bound the completion prompt list to prevent unbounded engine fan-out (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47845">#47845</a>); guard lm-format-enforcer regex compilation with a timeout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47595">#47595</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li>Transformers 5.13.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47867">#47867</a>), FlashInfer 0.6.14 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47669">#47669</a>), NIXL 1.3.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47559">#47559</a>), tpu-inference v0.24.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47835">#47835</a>), nvidia-cutlass-dsl 4.6.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47442">#47442</a>), vllm_xpu_kernels v0.1.11.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48942">#48942</a>).</li>
<li>FlashAttention 3 pinned to the torch stable-ABI commit (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47995">#47995</a>); ABI-stable FlashMLA build (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48174">#48174</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Deprecations &amp; Removals</h3>
<ul>
<li>Models removed: TeleChat (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47989">#47989</a>), Persimmon and Fuyu (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48096">#48096</a>).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.26.0rc1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.26.0rc1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>v0.26.0rc1</p>

---

### [v0.25.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.25.1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.25.1</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 2 commits from 2 contributors (1 new)!</p>
<p>v0.25.1 is a patch release containing two targeted bug fixes on top of v0.25.0.</p>
<h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Avoid blocking model launching when no system FFmpeg is available for TorchCodec</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47888">#47888</a>). Previously <code>import torchcodec</code> raised a <code>RuntimeError</code> at import time when system FFmpeg was missing, which blocked startup (e.g. <code>vllm serve Qwen/Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct</code>) even when TorchCodec was not in use. The error is now deferred to runtime so it only surfaces if TorchCodec is actually needed.</li>
<li><strong>Guard mixed-dtype allreduce RMSNorm quant fusions</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48330">#48330</a>). The fused FlashInfer allreduce + RMSNorm + static-quantization patterns could match graphs where the activation and RMSNorm weight dtypes differ (e.g. a BF16 residual stream with an FP32 Gemma/Qwen-style RMSNorm weight in NVFP4 models), corrupting the hidden state and producing garbage output such as repeated <code>!!!!!</code> tokens. A dtype-match guard now routes incompatible mixed-dtype graphs to the safe path, while same-dtype models retain the full allreduce + RMSNorm + quant fusion.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Contributors</h2>
<p><a class="user-mention notranslate" href="https://github.com/Isotr0py">@Isotr0py</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" href="https://github.com/hugo-cen">@hugo-cen</a></p>
<h2>New Contributors</h2>
<ul>
<li><a class="user-mention notranslate" href="https://github.com/hugo-cen">@hugo-cen</a> made their first contribution in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48330">#48330</a></li>
</ul>

### [v0.25.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.25.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.25.0 Release Notes</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 558 commits from 232 contributors (64 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 is now the default for all dense models</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44443">#44443</a>). Building on quantized-model support from the previous release, MRv2 is now the standard execution path, with new support for EVS (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46535">#46535</a>), realtime embeddings (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46762">#46762</a>), prefix caching for Mamba hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42406">#42406</a>), multimodal-prefix bidirectional attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46942">#46942</a>), and dynamic speculative decoding compatible with full CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45953">#45953</a>).</li>
<li><strong>PagedAttention has been removed</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47361">#47361</a>). The legacy attention implementation is deleted now that V1/MRv2 backends are the standard path.</li>
<li><strong>The Transformers modeling backend is now as fast as native vLLM</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47187">#47187</a>), and gained FP8 MoE support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46820">#46820</a>), CUDA graph + embed scaling fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48010">#48010</a>), and migration of GPTBigCode/Starcoder2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30966">#30966</a>) and RoBERTa (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47452">#47452</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New models</strong>: LLaVA-OneVision-2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44785">#44785</a>), Unlimited OCR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46564">#46564</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47102">#47102</a>), MOSS-Transcribe-Diarize (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47729">#47729</a>), openai/privacy-filter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41026">#41026</a>), and Hy3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47192">#47192</a>). GLM-5 / DeepSeek-V3.2 landed in the model zoo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46808">#46808</a>) with GLM-5.2 tuning, and MiniMax-M3 gained pipeline parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45810">#45810</a>) and NVFP4 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46756">#46756</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New Streaming Parser Engine</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46610">#46610</a>) — a unified tool-call/reasoning parsing framework, with a new Kimi k2.5/k2.6/k2.7 parser and ports of seed_oss (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46314">#46314</a>) and DeepSeek V4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45877">#45877</a>). The Rust frontend continues to mature with HTTPS/mTLS (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45890">#45890</a>), a DP supervisor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47076">#47076</a>), and profiler control routes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46306">#46306</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Universal speculative decoding for heterogeneous vocabularies (TLI)</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38174">#38174</a>), plus new DSpark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46995">#46995</a>) and DFlash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46770">#46770</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46853">#46853</a>) drafters.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>New models: LLaVA-OneVision-2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44785">#44785</a>), Unlimited OCR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46564">#46564</a>) with a Triton R-SWA backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47102">#47102</a>), MOSS-Transcribe-Diarize (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47729">#47729</a>), openai/privacy-filter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41026">#41026</a>), Hy3 with token-suffix and JSON Schema array support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47192">#47192</a>).</li>
<li>GLM-5 family: GLM-5 / DeepSeek-V3.2 added to the model zoo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46808">#46808</a>), GLM-5.2 FP32 gate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47410">#47410</a>), GLM MTP post-final-norm fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47448">#47448</a>), GLM4V startup fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47155">#47155</a>).</li>
<li>MiniMax-M3: pipeline parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45810">#45810</a>), streaming reasoning parsing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45718">#45718</a>), and <code>tok_sparse_select</code> from MSA replacing Triton kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47502">#47502</a>).</li>
<li>Transformers backend: now as fast as native vLLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47187">#47187</a>), FP8 MoE fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46820">#46820</a>), embed scaling + CUDA graph fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48010">#48010</a>), GPTBigCode/Starcoder2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30966">#30966</a>) and RoBERTa (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47452">#47452</a>) migration, M-RoPE <code>mm_token_type_ids</code> fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46552">#46552</a>), tied-embedding <code>lm_head.bias</code> fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46835">#46835</a>).</li>
<li>Voxtral: migrated to mistral-common 1.11.5 audio API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46705">#46705</a>) and realtime token-feedback hang fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44461">#44461</a>).</li>
<li>Gemma family: Gemma4 sliding-window/FA4 attention fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47217">#47217</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47332">#47332</a>), Gemma4 MTP quant_config fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47091">#47091</a>); DiffusionGemma tensor parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/45719">#45719</a>) and HF stability-window semantics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45965">#45965</a>).</li>
<li>Other fixes: MiniCPM-V 4.6 language-backbone LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46740">#46740</a>) and placeholder grid fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45918">#45918</a>), pooled Whisper sliding-window sizing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47071">#47071</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47437">#47437</a>), Mamba/Mamba2 checkpoint-without-<code>architectures</code> crash fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46037">#46037</a>), DeepSeek-V2 hidden-size and aux-hidden-state fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46986">#46986</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46973">#46973</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li>Model Runner V2: default for all dense models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44443">#44443</a>); EVS (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46535">#46535</a>), realtime embeddings (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46762">#46762</a>), Mamba hybrid prefix caching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42406">#42406</a>), multimodal-prefix bidirectional attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46942">#46942</a>), cross-attention warmup/block-table fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46753">#46753</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47308">#47308</a>), Mamba2 crash fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47428">#47428</a>), scheduling slot accounting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46974">#46974</a>), model-ref cleanup on shutdown (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47483">#47483</a>), bounded memory for large-logprobs requests (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46746">#46746</a>).</li>
<li>Speculative decoding: universal spec decode for heterogeneous vocabularies (TLI) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38174">#38174</a>); DSpark drafter + speculators checkpoint support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46995">#46995</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47093">#47093</a>); DFlash backend selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46770">#46770</a>), per-layer RMSNorm fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46761">#46761</a>), CPU support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44029">#44029</a>), SWA+DFlash for MiMo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46104">#46104</a>), Laguna XS.2.1 drafter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46853">#46853</a>); MTP for Bailing hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44880">#44880</a>); block verification for rejection sampling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46781">#46781</a>); reduced TP communication for draft tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46448">#46448</a>).</li>
<li>Sleep mode: pluggable sleep-mode backend abstraction (RFC <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/34303">#34303</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44074">#44074</a>) with communicator-agnostic capability flags (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47243">#47243</a>).</li>
<li>Attention: FlashAttention block-size restriction removed for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36701">#36701</a>), <code>FLASH_ATTN_MLA_SPARSE</code> Hopper sparse-MLA backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46189">#46189</a>), DCP + FP8 KV cache in MLA decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44044">#44044</a>), XQA decode kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43232">#43232</a>).</li>
<li>KV offloading: tiering metric plumbing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45959">#45959</a>), request lifecycle fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46284">#46284</a>), batched lookup in C (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46713">#46713</a>), <code>LookupResult</code> enum (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46363">#46363</a>).</li>
<li>Misc: <code>VLLM_GPU_SYNC_CHECK</code> env var (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44800">#44800</a>), VRAM semaphore infrastructure (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44465">#44465</a>), skip detokenization in online beam search (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46422">#46422</a>), several int32-overflow fixes in sampler/attention kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46560">#46560</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47383">#47383</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47671">#47671</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li>GLM-5.2 / DeepSeek: <code>fused_indexer_q_rope_quant</code> Triton kernel (1.9–3.3% E2E throughput) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46862">#46862</a>), reduce-scatter MoE all-reduce (3.1–3.2% E2E) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46635">#46635</a>), op fusion for GLM5/DSV3.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46876">#46876</a>), <code>token_to_req_indices</code> cache for DSv4 (5–6x kernel speedup) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47474">#47474</a>), better DSv4 MXFP8 kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47229">#47229</a>), redundant-op removal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47198">#47198</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46651">#46651</a>).</li>
<li>NVIDIA/Blackwell: FlashInfer fused all-reduce tuned for world_size=16 on GB300 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46392">#46392</a>), restored NVFP4 swizzled-scale zero-init to recover Blackwell decode throughput (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45739">#45739</a>), CuTeDSL/FA4-MLA warmup infrastructure (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46182">#46182</a>), skip cooperative top-K on SM120 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47164">#47164</a>), B12x backend for non-gated MoEs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43328">#43328</a>).</li>
<li>Kernels: Helion <code>fused_qk_norm_rope</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44010">#44010</a>) and <code>silu_and_mul_per_block_quant</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43994">#43994</a>), Triton MLA logits workspace (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46819">#46819</a>), swap-AB optimization for fused MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36559">#36559</a>), vectorized fp32 <code>moe_sum</code> supporting any top-k (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46643">#46643</a>), blocking CUDA events to avoid busy-polling the driver lock (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47081">#47081</a>).</li>
<li>AMD/ROCm: moved to torch 2.11 stable ABI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47128">#47128</a>); AITER FlashAttention MLA prefill backend <code>ROCM_AITER_FA</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45033">#45033</a>); fused shared-expert for GLM-4.5/6/7 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44313">#44313</a>) and MiniMax-M3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46474">#46474</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46545">#46545</a>); AITER MoE optimization for DeepSeek-V4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46122">#46122</a>); AITER custom all-reduce in CudaCommunicator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46065">#46065</a>); INT3 quantization for quickreduce (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45666">#45666</a>).</li>
<li>Intel XPU: W8A8 FP8 linear kernel with multi-granularity quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43645">#43645</a>), pipeline-parallel accuracy fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47253">#47253</a>), uniform-batch CUDA graph for FA2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46555">#46555</a>), route mm_prefix models to Triton attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47688">#47688</a>), C++ <code>get_memory_info</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47134">#47134</a>).</li>
<li>CPU: accelerated unquantized MoE for AArch64 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46353">#46353</a>), macOS/Apple Silicon hang fix via OpenMP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46769">#46769</a>) and broken-install fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47457">#47457</a>), compressed-tensor w8a8 int8 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42920">#42920</a>), Mamba ShortConv (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35059">#35059</a>), chunked prefill + prefix caching for Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46202">#46202</a>), faster gelu via tanh AOR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44639">#44639</a>).</li>
<li>RISC-V: RVV path for W4A8 INT4 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45269">#45269</a>), BF16 on VLEN=256 hardware (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45243">#45243</a>), reduced LMUL pressure in INT4 LUT dequant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47538">#47538</a>). POWER: fp16 support on PowerPC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46135">#46135</a>).</li>
<li>Platform: accelerator-agnostic <code>get_memory_info</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44825">#44825</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving &amp; Distributed</h3>
<ul>
<li>Sequence parallelism without requiring DP, 1.9–5.0% E2E throughput improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47070">#47070</a>).</li>
<li>Distributed: NCCL symmetric memory extended to AllGather and ReduceScatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46703">#46703</a>), FlashInfer all-reduce defaults to MNNVL on single node (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47219">#47219</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47589">#47589</a>), fault-tolerance backend to detect all2all peer faults and prevent corrupted output (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43637">#43637</a>).</li>
<li>Data parallel: throttle prefills based on local prefill work (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46532">#46532</a>), rotate load-balancer tie-break to avoid engine bias (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47420">#47420</a>), DP supervisor via the Rust frontend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47076">#47076</a>), DP MTP hang fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40589">#40589</a>).</li>
<li>PD disaggregation: secondary-tier implementation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42285">#42285</a>), Mooncake connector GDN (Qwen3.5) + MLA (DeepSeek-V4-Flash) support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46807">#46807</a>), NIXL Mamba1 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45019">#45019</a>), MultiConnector <code>kv_transfer_params</code> merging (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46777">#46777</a>), usage field exposed for disaggregated serving (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42748">#42748</a>).</li>
<li>DCP: FlashInfer MLA support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43729">#43729</a>), FLASHINFER_MLA_SPARSE support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46076">#46076</a>), LSE log-base fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47079">#47079</a>); Mooncake parallelized KV load (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45971">#45971</a>) and DCP&gt;1 lookup fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46855">#46855</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: stabilized high-throughput DBO for DP+EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46990">#46990</a>), EPLB for Quark OCP MXFP4 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47220">#47220</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li>2/3/5/6/7-bit pack-quantized weight-only inference (Humming) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46389">#46389</a>), Triton INT4 per-token-head KV cache quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40835">#40835</a>).</li>
<li>NVFP4: fused weight dequantization with compute in the MoE MLP Triton kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44667">#44667</a>), NVFP4 KV cache with skip-layers sliding window (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42890">#42890</a>), MiniMax-M3 ModelOpt NVFP4 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46756">#46756</a>).</li>
<li>FP8: weights padding for per-block online quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44763">#44763</a>); deprecated the old FP8 online MoE quantization class (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44514">#44514</a>).</li>
<li>Marlin: thread-tile padding extended to MoE (WNA16 + FP8/MXFP8) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45703">#45703</a>), int8 grouped WNA16 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47154">#47154</a>); FlashInfer MXINT4 MoE for gated SiLU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46518">#46518</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: W8A8 int-quant scheme-selection regression (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46860">#46860</a>), tied quantized embeddings for ModelOpt Gemma4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45544">#45544</a>), NVFP4+MTP crash on Qwen3Next (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46316">#46316</a>), ModelOpt mixed-precision for sparse configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47318">#47318</a>), CPU w4a8_int8 MoE path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46739">#46739</a>), actionable error on group-size/TP mismatch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/46230">#46230</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li>Streaming Parser Engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46610">#46610</a>): unified tool-call/reasoning parsing with a new Kimi k2.5/k2.6/k2.7 parser; ported seed_oss (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46314">#46314</a>) and DeepSeek V4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45877">#45877</a>).</li>
<li>OpenAI compatibility: Responses API namespace tools (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47024">#47024</a>), per-request timing <code>metrics</code> field on Chat/Completions responses (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46768">#46768</a>), token offsets on render endpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44226">#44226</a>), <code>return_loss_mask</code> for training-data generation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46846">#46846</a>), HTTP 422 for unprocessable image URLs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47165">#47165</a>).</li>
<li>gpt-oss / Harmony: dedicated Harmony renderer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46800">#46800</a>), <code>process_eos()</code> flush (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46437">#46437</a>), raw-output recovery on non-terminal parse (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47062">#47062</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47379">#47379</a>).</li>
<li>Rust frontend: static HTTPS and mTLS for HTTP and gRPC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45890">#45890</a>), DP supervisor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47076">#47076</a>), profiler control routes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46306">#46306</a>), <code>repetition_detection</code> sampling param (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46684">#46684</a>), unified/combined parser interface (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46583">#46583</a>), reduced multimodal tensor copies (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47581">#47581</a>), plus many parser and validation fixes.</li>
<li>Video: TorchCodec added as a video decoding backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46609">#46609</a>).</li>
<li>CLI/UX: TTFT and TPS printing in <code>vllm chat</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46775">#46775</a>), <code>model_class_overrides</code> for development/debugging (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47148">#47148</a>).</li>
<li>Tooling/validation: many tool-parser fixes (Kimi K2 IDs <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46344">#46344</a>, PoolsideV1 <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46486">#46486</a>/<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47311">#47311</a>, non-ASCII arguments <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46308">#46308</a>, <code>thinking_token_budget</code> re-entry <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43757">#43757</a>); rejection of invalid config values (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44070">#44070</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44002">#44002</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46612">#46612</a>) and degenerate <code>structured_outputs</code> that crash EngineCore (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45346">#45346</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>Prevent image decompression-bomb OOM denial of service (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47010">#47010</a>).</li>
<li>Prevent an infinite loop in <code>split_audio</code> with NaN audio samples (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46463">#46463</a>).</li>
<li>Bound tokenizer work when an explicit <code>truncation_side</code> is set (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47007">#47007</a>).</li>
<li>Block request-level GPU video backend selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47259">#47259</a>).</li>
<li>Document the gRPC interface as insecure, for private use only (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45903">#45903</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li>FlashInfer 0.6.13 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46683">#46683</a>), tpu-inference v0.23.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46568">#46568</a>), aiter 0.1.16.post2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46692">#46692</a>), vllm_xpu_kernels v0.1.10.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46607">#46607</a>), huggingface-hub v1.22.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47551">#47551</a>).</li>
<li>DeepGEMM updated to enable SM120 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47304">#47304</a>), FlashAttention 3 built against the torch stable API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46644">#46644</a>), Rust frontend TLS switched from rustls to native-tls/OpenSSL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46696">#46696</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Deprecations &amp; Removals</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>PagedAttention deleted</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47361">#47361</a>).</li>
<li>Models removed: Baichuan (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46362">#46362</a>), Aquila (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46605">#46605</a>), Grok (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46706">#46706</a>), Tarsier / Tarsier2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47143">#47143</a>), AyaVision / MusicFlamingo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47263">#47263</a>), Mantis (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46806">#46806</a>).</li>
<li>Deprecated the old FP8 online MoE quantization class (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44514">#44514</a>); legacy <code>api_server.py</code> moved to the examples directory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46783">#46783</a>); <code>gptq_marlin</code> removed from supported ROCm quant schemes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46655">#46655</a>).</li>
</ul>
